# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-09-15 through 2026-09-21  
**Updated:** 2026-09-15T10:53:51+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-01-13

## Best date plans

### 2026-09-17 — Winner + FUMBL Fest

**Dinner, then FUMBL Fest.** Bakery and American cafe at Winner. It is 1.9 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Doors 6:30 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=367+7th+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=290+Conover+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://ticketstripe.com/fumblfest) · [Restaurant](https://www.winner.nyc/)

### 2026-09-15 — Movies in the Square: High School Musical 2 and Fame + The Four Horsemen

**Movies in the Square: High School Musical 2 and Fame, then dinner.** Wine bar and seasonal American at The Four Horsemen. It is a 12-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=15+River+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&destination=295+Grand+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.dominopark.com/events/movies-in-the-square-high-school-musical-2-and-fame) · [Restaurant](https://www.fourhorsemenbk.com/)

### 2026-09-16 — Gather Round: Let's Dance at Manhattan West - Sekou McMiller & Friends + Yellow Rose

**Gather Round: Let's Dance at Manhattan West - Sekou McMiller & Friends, then dinner.** Tex-Mex at Yellow Rose. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 5 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=385+9th+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&destination=102+3rd+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://manhattanwestnyc.com/event/gather-round-lets-dance-2026/2026-09-16/) · [Restaurant](https://www.yellowrosenyc.com/)

### 2026-09-19 — Hester Street Fair Summer Market + Dhamaka

**Hester Street Fair Summer Market, then a meal.** Regional Indian at Dhamaka. It is a 3-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=26+Essex+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10002&destination=119+Delancey+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10002&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://hesterstreetfair.com/highlights/2026-summer-markets/) · [Restaurant](https://www.dhamaka.nyc/)

### 2026-09-19 — Mortal Matters: Echoes and Epitaphs + Winner

**Mortal Matters: Echoes and Epitaphs, then a meal.** Bakery and American cafe at Winner. It is a 16-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 1 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=750+Fifth+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11232&destination=367+7th+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.green-wood.com/event/mortal-matters/) · [Restaurant](https://www.winner.nyc/)

### 2026-09-16 — Gather Round: Mambo Dance Party + Thai Diner

**Gather Round: Mambo Dance Party, then dinner.** Thai diner at Thai Diner. It is 2.2 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 5 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=385+9th+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&destination=186+Mott+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10012&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://manhattanwestnyc.com/event/gather-round-lets-dance-2026/2026-09-16/) · [Restaurant](https://www.thaidiner.com/)

### 2026-09-16 — Yellow Rose + The Quiet Beekeeper | New Nordic Cinema

**Dinner, then The Quiet Beekeeper | New Nordic Cinema.** Tex-Mex at Yellow Rose. It is 1.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. The Quiet Beekeeper | New Nordic Cinema starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=102+3rd+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&destination=58+Park+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10016&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.scandinaviahouse.org/event/the-quiet-beekeeper-new-nordic-cinema/) · [Restaurant](https://www.yellowrosenyc.com/)

### 2026-09-17 — Dhamaka + Oculus Outdoors: Zoolander

**Dinner, then Oculus Outdoors: Zoolander.** Regional Indian at Dhamaka. It is 1.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Oculus Outdoors: Zoolander starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=119+Delancey+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10002&destination=50+Church+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10007&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.explorewtc.com/en/local/things-to-do/events/oculus-outdoors-2026.html) · [Restaurant](https://www.dhamaka.nyc/)

## Where to eat

Verified current restaurant pages and captured menu evidence, keeping useful NYC dining cards while removing duplicate offers and unsupported exact claims.

Dining research: 2026-09-15T10:39:17+00:00

Michelin Guide page fetches for The Modern, Aquavit and Le Bernardin returned no readable content in this pass. I retained only award facts supported by restaurant-owned captured pages: The Modern's homepage says it holds two Michelin stars, and Aquavit's homepage says Two Michelin Stars, 2026 New York City Guide. Le Bernardin menu/award details were not retained because the captured official menu page was blocked/page-shell only.

### Aperitivo menu in the bar and lounge

Deals & happy hours — Ci Siamo

A verified weekday bar-and-lounge aperitivo window with Italian snacks such as pizza bianca, gnocco fritto, mortadella, prosciutto, pizzette, tuna tonnato, and smashed potatoes.

Price: Aperitivo items checked: $14-$27; examples include Mortadella Con Pistacchio $14, Pizza Bianca $15, Gnocco Fritto $16, Smashed Potatoes $16, Tuna Tonnato $26, Caramelized Onion Torta $27.  
Schedule: Monday-Friday, 3:00 PM-4:30 PM; served in the Bar & Lounge.  
Conditions: Official page says to inform the team of food allergies. Tax, tip, and service charges not stated on the checked page. Reservation need unknown for the aperitivo window; main reservation link is OpenTable.  
[Source](https://www.cisiamo.com/menus/)

### Lunch with lower-priced pastas and mains

More restaurants — Ci Siamo

Useful current lunch option near Hudson Yards with live-fire Italian cooking and several pastas in the $25-$35 range; this is regular menu intel, not a special event.

Price: Lunch examples checked: Tagliatelle $25, Tomato Orecchiette $27, Stracci $29, Rigatoni Alla Gricia $31, Cavatelli Allo Scoglio $35, Roasted Chicken $32, Wood Fired Whole Trout $39.  
Schedule: Lunch menu verified on the official menu page; exact lunch days/hours were not visible in the checked captured text.  
Conditions: Ordinary lunch menu, not a special-menu event. Tax, tip, service charges and exact lunch hours were not stated in the checked captured menu text. Allergy note present.  
[Source](https://www.cisiamo.com/menus/)

### Midday bar-bites window

Deals & happy hours — Union Square Cafe

A useful between-meals window with a dedicated Midday menu, including snacks, seafood, salads, small pasta portions, and a burger.

Price: Midday examples checked: Duck Liver Profiterole $6, Garlic Potato Chips $9, Spicy Tuna Tater Tots $12, Bibb Salad $19, Assam Fluke $23, Rigatoni alla Norma 24 | 34, 19th Street Burger $32.  
Schedule: Daily, 2:30 PM-5:00 PM.  
Conditions: Official page lists the Midday time window. Some raw-bar items are marked with an asterisk/raw-food warning elsewhere on the page. Tax, tip, and service charges not stated.  
[Source](https://www.unionsquarecafe.com/menus/)

### Dining Room lunch three-course option

Special menus — Gramercy Tavern

A high-quality but less expensive way into Gramercy Tavern’s Dining Room: lunch can be à la carte or a three-course option.

## Book ahead

#### [Marcel Duchamp](https://gagosian.com/exhibitions/2026/marcel-duchamp/)

2026-09-26 · 10:00 AM · art · Free · Gagosian, 980 Madison Avenue · Upper East Side · Manhattan · Score 74.0  
Gagosian's presentation of key Marcel Duchamp works inaugurates its new ground-floor space at 980 Madison, returning iconic readymades to the building where these editions made their American debut in 1965.
**Timing:** Final day of the exhibition; gallery hours should be confirmed before going.
**Practical note:** Commercial gallery visit; confirm same-day hours before traveling.
**Why now:** The exhibition closes September 26, just outside the seven-day window.
**Hot booking alert:** Gagosian lists the exhibition as extended only through September 26, making this the final post-seven-day chance to see it.
[Official](https://gagosian.com/exhibitions/2026/marcel-duchamp/) · [Tickets / booking](https://gagosian.com/exhibitions/2026/marcel-duchamp/)

#### [Robert Rauschenberg: Important Early Works from the Cy Twombly Foundation](https://gagosian.com/exhibitions/2026/robert-rauschenberg-important-early-works-from-the-cy-twombly-foundation/)

2026-09-26 · 10:00 AM · art · Free · Gagosian, 980 Madison Avenue · Upper East Side · Manhattan · Score 74.0  
Six early Rauschenberg works from the Cy Twombly Foundation, including one of his earliest surviving sculptures and a recognized blueprint photogram, are on view during Rauschenberg's centennial year.
**Timing:** Final day of the exhibition; gallery hours should be confirmed before going.
**Practical note:** Same building as Gagosian's Duchamp exhibition, so the two closing alerts can be combined in one visit.
**Why now:** The exhibition closes September 26, beyond the next-seven-days lane but close enough to miss.
**Hot booking alert:** Gagosian lists the presentation as extended only through September 26, so the remaining viewing window is short and fixed.
